        Idea:
Multi-select feature parity: the bulk properties panel should expose all the same settings as single-clip editing (effects, position/crop, text, transitions, entrance/exit/continuous animations, volume slider, from/to timing, etc.), not just opacity/speed/mute. Also ensure all single-clip operations work with multi-selection where it makes sense: arrow key track movement, context menu operations (copy, split-at-playhead for each), and live visual feedback during multi-drag. The BulkClipPanel in PropertiesPanel.tsx currently only shows 3 fields — it needs to match the full ClipPanel feature set with mixed-value handling.

  "meta_commentary": "**Execution guidance:**\n\n**Parallelism:** T1, T2, T3, T6, T7 are independent \u2014 execute in parallel. T4 depends on T1-T3 (needs the utility types and handler signatures). T5 depends on T4 (passes computed props to the new BulkClipPanel). T8-T9 are sequential validation after all code.\n\n**The shallow-merge bug is the #1 correctness risk.** The entire plan's architecture revolves around the patch factory pattern in T2. When reviewing T4, verify that EVERY nested-field edit (entrance, exit, continuous, text fontSize, text color) goes through `onChangeDeep`, not `onChange`. Scalar fields (opacity, speed, volume, x, y, width, height, from, to) are safe to use `onChange`.\n\n**Tab visibility correction (DECISION-006):** The plan's Step 1.4 says Position is shown \"if ANY selected clip is non-text on a visual track.\" This is WRONG per the gate warning. ClipPanel.tsx:41-42 shows Position for text clips too. The correct rule for getBulkVisibleTabs is: Position shown if ANY clip is on a visual track (regardless of clipType). The executor MUST correct this.\n\n**Unresolved design decisions made here:**\n- Cross-kind arrow movement: Each kind-group moves independently (not all-or-nothing across kinds). This is more useful than blocking the entire operation when a mixed selection can't fully move.\n- Bulk Source In/Out: Included as scalar fields. Applying the same source trim to all clips is a valid use case (e.g., skip first 2 seconds of all clips).\n\n**What NOT to build:** Crop controls (in OverlayEditor), transition controls (don't exist anywhere), copy/paste (no infrastructure), undo/redo (no system), start time bulk edit (destructive stacking).\n\n**Extracting shared code:** FieldLabel and NO_EFFECT/TAB_COLUMNS_CLASS from ClipPanel.tsx will be needed by BulkClipPanel. Either extract to a shared file or co-locate in PropertiesPanel.tsx. Don't duplicate the effect type arrays \u2014 import from `effects.ts`.\n\n**Wiring checklist for new handlers:** Each new handler must appear in: (1) useClipEditing.ts return object, (2) UseClipEditingResult interface, (3) useTimelineState.ts editor useMemo, (4) useTimelineState.ts useMemo deps array, (5) TimelineEditorContextValue in useTimelineState.types.ts. Missing any of these 5 causes a silent failure where the handler is undefined at runtime."

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-panel-tabs: text clip multi-selections would miss the position tab under the plan's stated rule. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] properties-panel-scope: crop and transition controls are not included in the bulk panel, narrowing the original idea's scope. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] tool-error-propagation: a blanket catch in executetool would silently downgrade db/persistence failures into ordinary tool results, changing the session error/http 500 behavior. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

Multi-select feature parity: the bulk properties panel should expose all the same settings as single-clip editing (effects, position/crop, text, transitions, entrance/exit/continuous animations, volume slider, from/to timing, etc.), not just opacity/speed/mute. Also ensure all single-clip operations work with multi-selection where it makes sense: arrow key track movement, context menu operations (copy, split-at-playhead for each), and live visual feedback during multi-drag. The BulkClipPanel in PropertiesPanel.tsx currently only shows 3 fields — it needs to match the full ClipPanel feature set with mixed-value handling.

- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] properties-panel-scope: crop and transition controls are not included in the bulk panel, narrowing the original idea's scope. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] tool-error-propagation: a blanket catch in executetool would silently downgrade db/persistence failures into ordinary tool results, changing the session error/http 500 behavior. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

    "[DECISION-006] Position tab must be shown for ALL visual-track clips including text. The plan's Step 1.4 incorrectly says 'non-text on a visual track' \u2014 this must be corrected to 'any clip on a visual track'. Reference: ClipPanel.tsx:41-42 returns position for text clips.",
    "[DEBT] bulk-panel-tabs: Ensure text clip multi-selections get the Position tab. This was flagged as existing debt \u2014 the implementation must not make it worse.",

    "[SCOPE] Bulk Source In/Out: These are scalar fields that apply the same value to all clips. This is potentially useful (e.g., 'trim all to start at 2s') but could surprise users. Include them but note in the UI that the value applies to all clips.",
